- {'document_excerpt': {'status': 'found', 'title': 'ORCA 6.1 Density Functional Theory', 'url': 'https://www.faccts.de/docs/orca/6.1/manual/contents/modelchemistries/DensityFunctionalTheory.html', 'document_id': 'orca_dft', 'index_version': 'knowledge-index-3', 'index_sha256': 'c204125e1f3c4d5aca3d2893a6525b0b0219450ca1b4a6a91e69becfe1dcaaf2', 'document_version': '6.1', 'kind': 'official_document', 'rendering_version': 'html-readable-2', 'published_at': None, 'accessed_at': '2026-10-20T11:43:49.964535+00:00', 'query': 'B3LYP hybrid functional dispersion correction', 'document_sha256': '94424fff221bde28abf4a32b7f1649771dcfde5585360082816107d4a6032c0c', 'excerpt': 'The correct choice of the local part of the correlation functional is crucial to ensure reproducibility of the results. In ORCA, almost all functionals choose PWLDA[197] as the underlying LDA functional as it represents a slightly better fit to the uniform electron gas than VWN. For VWN (Vosko, Wilk, and Nusair[196]), several parameterizations exist. In their classic paper give two sets of parameters – one in the main body (parametrization of RPA results; known as VWN-III) and one in their table 5 of correlation energies (parametrization of the Ceperley/Alder Monte Carlo results; known as VWN-V). The results from PW-LDA are very similar to those of VWN-V, whereas VWN-III yields quite different values. Over the years, the usage of different parameterizations for the same functional name throughout different quantum chemistry programs caused some confusion and deviating results. A prominent example is the different implementation of the B3LYP hybrid functional within ORCA and Gaussian. While ORCA uses VNW-V consistent with the TURBOMOLE program[222, 223, 224], the Gaussian program[225] employs VWN-III. Using the LDAOpt keyword, both variants can be constructed:\n\nB3LYP [230] B3LYP 20 The popular B3LYP functional (20% HF exchange) as defined in the TurboMole program system (LDAOPT C_VWN5)\n\nB3LYP hyb_gga_xc_b3lyp5', 'excerpt_sha256': 'b29876c137a3faa3b54688fd43c7f67f1d7cc38ea2595d8df9a228df66ef383d', 'coverage': {'complete': False, 'scope': 'bounded matched document paragraphs and table rows', 'paragraph_indices': [64, 136, 398], 'paragraph_count': 502, 'table_row_labels': {'136': 'B3LYP [230]', '398': 'B3LYP'}}, 'scientific_qualification': False, 'retrieval': 'versioned official-document index; one HTTPS GET', 'artifact_ids': ['artifact_55b02ae2139b41e08a9c0cc98de5c075'], 'document_artifact_id': 'artifact_55b02ae2139b41e08a9c0cc98de5c075', 'document_archive_complete': True, 'document_bytes': 283823, 'source': {'run_id': 'run_35cc4bcc85f3492babda693160dd846f', 'call_id': 'call_cfd1df77d064454e807c77e45a53337d', 'tool': 'knowledge.search', 'url': 'https://www.faccts.de/docs/orca/6.1/manual/contents/modelchemistries/DensityFunctionalTheory.html', 'document_id': 'orca_dft', 'document_version': '6.1', 'accessed_at': '2026-10-20T11:43:49.964535+00:00', 'byte_semantics': 'HTTP content-decoded body before UTF-8 text decoding; packaged guides use UTF-8 bytes', 'scientific_qualification': False, 'files': {'document.html': {'artifact_id': 'artifact_55b02ae2139b41e08a9c0cc98de5c075', 'sha256': '94424fff221bde28abf4a32b7f1649771dcfde5585360082816107d4a6032c0c'}}}}}

not a scientific qualification', 'lines': [{'line': 1, 'text': 'import os, json, math, shutil, re'}, {'line': 2, 'text': 'from orca_task_sdk import run_orca'}, {'line': 3, 'text': ''}, {'line': 4, 'text': 'INITIAL_XYZ = "initial.xyz"'}, {'line': 5, 'text': 'ANGLES = [-180, -120, -60, 0, 60, 120, 180]'}, {'line': 6, 'text': 'LABELS = {-180:"m180", -120:"m120", -60:"m60", 0:"0", 60:"60", 120:"120", 180:"180"}'}, {'line': 7, 'text': 'HARTREE_TO_KJ = 2625.499639'}, {'line': 8, 'text': ''}, {'line': 9, 'text': ''}, {'line': 10, 'text': 'def parse_xyz(path):'}, {'line': 11, 'text': '    with open(path, "r") as f:'}, {'line': 12, 'text': '        lines = [ln.rstrip("\\n") for ln in f]'}, {'line': 13, 'text': '    n = int(lines[0].split()[0])'}, {'line': 14, 'text': '    atoms = []'}, {'line': 15, 'text': '    for ln in lines[2:2+n]:'}, {'line': 16, 'text': '        p = ln.split()'}, {'line': 17, 'text': '        if len(p) < 4:'}, {'line': 18, 'text': '            continue'}, {'line': 19, 'text': '        atoms.append((p[0], float(p[1]), float(p[2]), float(p[3])))'}, {'line': 20, 'text': '    return atoms'}, {'line': 21, 'text': ''}, {'line': 22, 'text': ''}, {'line': 23, 'text': 'def sub(a,b): return (a[0]-b[0], a[1]-b[1], a[2]-b[2])'}, {'line': 24, 'text': 'def dot(a,b): return a[0]*b[0]+a[1]*b[1]+a[2]*b[2]'}, {'line': 25, 'text': 'def cross(a,b): return (a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1], a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2], a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0])'}, {'line': 26, 'text': 'def norm(a):'}, {'line': 27, 'text': '    n = math.sqrt(dot(a,a))'}, {'line': 28, 'text': '    return (a[0]/n, a[1]/n, a[2]/n)'}, {'line': 29, 'text': ''}, {'line': 30, 'text': ''}, {'line': 31, 'text': 'def dihedral_deg(a,b,c,d):'}, {'line': 32, 'text': '    p0 = (a[1],a[2],a[3]); p1 = (b[1],b[2],b[3]); p2 = (c[1],c[2],c[3]); p3 = (d[1],d[2],d[3])'}, {'line': 33, 'text': '    b0 = sub(p1,p0); b1 = sub(p2,p1); b2 = sub(p3,p2); b1n = norm(b1)'}, {'line': 34, 'text': '    v = sub(b0, tuple(b1n[k]*dot(b0,b1n) for k in range(3)))'}, {'line': 35, 'text': '    w = sub(b2, tuple(b1n[k]*dot(b2,b1n) for k in range(3)))'}, {'line': 36, 'text': '    x = dot(v,w); y = dot(cross(b1n,v),w)'}, {'line': 37, 'text': '    return math.degrees(math.atan2(y,x))'}, {'line': 38, 'text': ''}, {'line': 39, 'text': ''}, {'line': 40, 'text': 'def find_dihedral_indices(atoms):'}, {'line': 41, 'text': '    carbons = [i for i,a in enumerate(atoms) if a[0].upper() == "C"]'}, {'line': 42, 'text': '    adj = {i:[] for i in carbons}'}, {'line': 43, 'text': '    for ii,i in enumerate(carbons):'}, {'line': 44, 'text': '        for j in carbons[ii+1:]:'}, {'line': 45, 'text': '            d = math.sqrt(sum((atoms[i][k+1]-atoms[j][k+1])**2 for k in range(3)))'}, {'line': 46, 'text': '            if d <= 1.75:'}, {'line': 47, 'text': '                adj[i].append(j); adj[j].append(i)'}, {'line': 48, 'text': '    best = []'}, {'line': 49, 'text': '    for s in carbons:'}, {'line': 50, 'text': '        stack = [(s, [s])]'}, {'line': 51, 'text': '        while stack:'}, {'line': 52, 'text': '            cur, path = stack.pop()'}, {'line': 53, 'text': '            if len(path) > len(best):'}, {'line': 54, 'text': '                best = path'}, {'line': 55, 'text': '            for nxt in adj[cur]:'}, {'line': 56, 'text': '                if nxt not in path:'}, {'line': 57, 'text': '                    stack.append((nxt, path+[nxt]))'}, {'line': 58, 'text': '    if len(best) < 4:'}, {'line': 59, 'text': '        raise RuntimeError("Could not find a four-carbon chain; carbons=%s adj=%s" % (carbons, adj))'}, {'line': 60, 'text': '    return best[:4]'}, {'line': 61, 'text': ''}, {'line': 62, 'text': ''}, {'line': 63, 'text': 'def write_input(angle, idxs):'}, {'line': 64, 'text': '    i,j,k,l = idxs'}, {'line': 65, 'text': '    inp_name = "scan_%s.inp" % LABELS[angle]'}, {'line': 66, 'text': '    lines = ['}, {'line': 67, 'text': '      "! B3LYP def2-TZVP RKS Opt NoAutoStart",'}, {'line': 68, 'text': '      "%maxcore 192",'}, {'line': 69, 'text': '      "%scf",'}, {'line': 70, 'text': '      "  MaxIter 256",'}, {'line': 71, 'text': '      "  AutoTRAH false",'}, {'line': 72, 'text': '      "end",'}, {'line': 73, 'text': '      "%geom",'}, {'line': 74, 'text': '      "  MaxIter 128",'}, {'line': 75, 'text': '      "  Constraints",'}, {'line': 76, 'text': '      "    { D %d %d %d %d %.1f C }" % (i,j,k,l,angle),'}, {'line': 77, 'text': '      "  end",'}, {'line': 78, 'text': '      "end",'}, {'line': 79, 'text': '      "* xyzfile 0 1 initial.xyz",'}, {'line': 80, 'text': '    ]'}, '[bounded report: additional items omitted]'], 'returned_bytes': 10136, 'status': 'observed', 'coverage': {'version': 'evidence-coverage-1', 'requested': {'kind': 'lines', 'start': 1, 'stop': 201}, 'returned': {'kind': 'lines', 'start': 1, 'stop': 194}, 'complete': True, 'truncated': False, 'reason': None, 'next_cursor': None}}}

not a scientific qualification', 'lines': [{'line': 1, 'text': 'import os, json, math, shutil, re'}, {'line': 2, 'text': 'from orca_task_sdk import run_orca'}, {'line': 3, 'text': ''}, {'line': 4, 'text': 'INITIAL_XYZ = "initial.xyz"'}, {'line': 5, 'text': 'ANGLES = [-180, -120, -60, 0, 60, 120, 180]'}, {'line': 6, 'text': 'LABELS = {-180:"m180", -120:"m120", -60:"m60", 0:"0", 60:"60", 120:"120", 180:"180"}'}, {'line': 7, 'text': 'HARTREE_TO_KJ = 2625.499639'}, {'line': 8, 'text': ''}, {'line': 9, 'text': ''}, {'line': 10, 'text': 'def parse_xyz(path):'}, {'line': 11, 'text': '    with open(path, "r") as f:'}, {'line': 12, 'text': '        lines = [ln.rstrip("\\n") for ln in f]'}, {'line': 13, 'text': '    n = int(lines[0].split()[0])'}, {'line': 14, 'text': '    atoms = []'}, {'line': 15, 'text': '    for ln in lines[2:2+n]:'}, {'line': 16, 'text': '        p = ln.split()'}, {'line': 17, 'text': '        if len(p) < 4:'}, {'line': 18, 'text': '            continue'}, {'line': 19, 'text': '        atoms.append((p[0], float(p[1]), float(p[2]), float(p[3])))'}, {'line': 20, 'text': '    return atoms'}, {'line': 21, 'text': ''}, {'line': 22, 'text': ''}, {'line': 23, 'text': 'def sub(a,b): return (a[0]-b[0], a[1]-b[1], a[2]-b[2])'}, {'line': 24, 'text': 'def dot(a,b): return a[0]*b[0]+a[1]*b[1]+a[2]*b[2]'}, {'line': 25, 'text': 'def cross(a,b): return (a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1], a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2], a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0])'}, {'line': 26, 'text': 'def norm(a):'}, {'line': 27, 'text': '    n = math.sqrt(dot(a,a))'}, {'line': 28, 'text': '    return (a[0]/n, a[1]/n, a[2]/n)'}, {'line': 29, 'text': ''}, {'line': 30, 'text': ''}, {'line': 31, 'text': 'def dihedral_deg(a,b,c,d):'}, {'line': 32, 'text': '    p0 = (a[1],a[2],a[3]); p1 = (b[1],b[2],b[3]); p2 = (c[1],c[2],c[3]); p3 = (d[1],d[2],d[3])'}, {'line': 33, 'text': '    b0 = sub(p1,p0); b1 = sub(p2,p1); b2 = sub(p3,p2); b1n = norm(b1)'}, {'line': 34, 'text': '    v = sub(b0, tuple(b1n[k]*dot(b0,b1n) for k in range(3)))'}, {'line': 35, 'text': '    w = sub(b2, tuple(b1n[k]*dot(b2,b1n) for k in range(3)))'}, {'line': 36, 'text': '    x = dot(v,w); y = dot(cross(b1n,v),w)'}, {'line': 37, 'text': '    return math.degrees(math.atan2(y,x))'}, {'line': 38, 'text': ''}, {'line': 39, 'text': ''}, {'line': 40, 'text': 'def find_dihedral_indices(atoms):'}, {'line': 41, 'text': '    carbons = [i for i,a in enumerate(atoms) if a[0].upper() == "C"]'}, {'line': 42, 'text': '    adj = {i:[] for i in carbons}'}, {'line': 43, 'text': '    for ii,i in enumerate(carbons):'}, {'line': 44, 'text': '        for j in carbons[ii+1:]:'}, {'line': 45, 'text': '            d = math.sqrt(sum((atoms[i][k+1]-atoms[j][k+1])**2 for k in range(3)))'}, {'line': 46, 'text': '            if d <= 1.75:'}, {'line': 47, 'text': '                adj[i].append(j); adj[j].append(i)'}, {'line': 48, 'text': '    best = []'}, {'line': 49, 'text': '    for s in carbons:'}, {'line': 50, 'text': '        stack = [(s, [s])]'}, {'line': 51, 'text': '        while stack:'}, {'line': 52, 'text': '            cur, path = stack.pop()'}, {'line': 53, 'text': '            if len(path) > len(best):'}, {'line': 54, 'text': '                best = path'}, {'line': 55, 'text': '            for nxt in adj[cur]:'}, {'line': 56, 'text': '                if nxt not in path:'}, {'line': 57, 'text': '                    stack.append((nxt, path+[nxt]))'}, {'line': 58, 'text': '    if len(best) < 4:'}, {'line': 59, 'text': '        raise RuntimeError("Could not find a four-carbon chain; carbons=%s adj=%s" % (carbons, adj))'}, {'line': 60, 'text': '    return best[:4]'}, {'line': 61, 'text': ''}, {'line': 62, 'text': ''}, {'line': 63, 'text': 'def write_input(angle, idxs):'}, {'line': 64, 'text': '    i,j,k,l = idxs'}, {'line': 65, 'text': '    inp_name = "scan_%s.inp" % LABELS[angle]'}, {'line': 66, 'text': '    lines = ['}, {'line': 67, 'text': '      "! B3LYP def2-TZVP RKS Opt NoAutoStart",'}, {'line': 68, 'text': '      "%maxcore 192",'}, {'line': 69, 'text': '      "%scf",'}, {'line': 70, 'text': '      "  MaxIter 256",'}, {'line': 71, 'text': '      "  AutoTRAH false",'}, {'line': 72, 'text': '      "end",'}, {'line': 73, 'text': '      "%geom",'}, {'line': 74, 'text': '      "  MaxIter 128",'}, {'line': 75, 'text': '      "  Constraints",'}, {'line': 76, 'text': '      "    { D %d %d %d %d %.1f C }" % (i,j,k,l,angle),'}, {'line': 77, 'text': '      "  end",'}, {'line': 78, 'text': '      "end",'}, {'line': 79, 'text': '      "* xyzfile 0 1 initial.xyz",'}, {'line': 80, 'text': '    ]'}, '[bounded report: additional items omitted]'], 'returned_bytes': 10136, 'status': 'observed', 'coverage': {'version': 'evidence-coverage-1', 'requested': {'kind': 'lines', 'start': 1, 'stop': 201}, 'returned': {'kind': 'lines', 'start': 1, 'stop': 194}, 'complete': True, 'truncated': False, 'reason': None, 'next_cursor': None}}}

not a scientific qualification', 'lines': [{'line': 1, 'text': 'import os, json, math, shutil, re'}, {'line': 2, 'text': 'from orca_task_sdk import run_orca'}, {'line': 3, 'text': ''}, {'line': 4, 'text': 'INITIAL_XYZ = "initial.xyz"'}, {'line': 5, 'text': 'ANGLES = [-180, -120, -60, 0, 60, 120, 180]'}, {'line': 6, 'text': 'LABELS = {-180:"m180", -120:"m120", -60:"m60", 0:"0", 60:"60", 120:"120", 180:"180"}'}, {'line': 7, 'text': 'HARTREE_TO_KJ = 2625.499639'}, {'line': 8, 'text': ''}, {'line': 9, 'text': ''}, {'line': 10, 'text': 'def parse_xyz(path):'}, {'line': 11, 'text': '    with open(path, "r") as f:'}, {'line': 12, 'text': '        lines = [ln.rstrip("\\n") for ln in f]'}, {'line': 13, 'text': '    n = int(lines[0].split()[0])'}, {'line': 14, 'text': '    atoms = []'}, {'line': 15, 'text': '    for ln in lines[2:2+n]:'}, {'line': 16, 'text': '        p = ln.split()'}, {'line': 17, 'text': '        if len(p) < 4:'}, {'line': 18, 'text': '            continue'}, {'line': 19, 'text': '        atoms.append((p[0], float(p[1]), float(p[2]), float(p[3])))'}, {'line': 20, 'text': '    return atoms'}, {'line': 21, 'text': ''}, {'line': 22, 'text': ''}, {'line': 23, 'text': 'def sub(a,b): return (a[0]-b[0], a[1]-b[1], a[2]-b[2])'}, {'line': 24, 'text': 'def dot(a,b): return a[0]*b[0]+a[1]*b[1]+a[2]*b[2]'}, {'line': 25, 'text': 'def cross(a,b): return (a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1], a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2], a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0])'}, {'line': 26, 'text': 'def norm(a):'}, {'line': 27, 'text': '    n = math.sqrt(dot(a,a))'}, {'line': 28, 'text': '    return (a[0]/n, a[1]/n, a[2]/n)'}, {'line': 29, 'text': ''}, {'line': 30, 'text': ''}, {'line': 31, 'text': 'def dihedral_deg(a,b,c,d):'}, {'line': 32, 'text': '    p0 = (a[1],a[2],a[3]); p1 = (b[1],b[2],b[3]); p2 = (c[1],c[2],c[3]); p3 = (d[1],d[2],d[3])'}, {'line': 33, 'text': '    b0 = sub(p1,p0); b1 = sub(p2,p1); b2 = sub(p3,p2); b1n = norm(b1)'}, {'line': 34, 'text': '    v = sub(b0, tuple(b1n[k]*dot(b0,b1n) for k in range(3)))'}, {'line': 35, 'text': '    w = sub(b2, tuple(b1n[k]*dot(b2,b1n) for k in range(3)))'}, {'line': 36, 'text': '    x = dot(v,w); y = dot(cross(b1n,v),w)'}, {'line': 37, 'text': '    return math.degrees(math.atan2(y,x))'}, {'line': 38, 'text': ''}, {'line': 39, 'text': ''}, {'line': 40, 'text': 'def find_dihedral_indices(atoms):'}, {'line': 41, 'text': '    carbons = [i for i,a in enumerate(atoms) if a[0].upper() == "C"]'}, {'line': 42, 'text': '    adj = {i:[] for i in carbons}'}, {'line': 43, 'text': '    for ii,i in enumerate(carbons):'}, {'line': 44, 'text': '        for j in carbons[ii+1:]:'}, {'line': 45, 'text': '            d = math.sqrt(sum((atoms[i][k+1]-atoms[j][k+1])**2 for k in range(3)))'}, {'line': 46, 'text': '            if d <= 1.75:'}, {'line': 47, 'text': '                adj[i].append(j); adj[j].append(i)'}, {'line': 48, 'text': '    best = []'}, {'line': 49, 'text': '    for s in carbons:'}, {'line': 50, 'text': '        stack = [(s, [s])]'}, {'line': 51, 'text': '        while stack:'}, {'line': 52, 'text': '            cur, path = stack.pop()'}, {'line': 53, 'text': '            if len(path) > len(best):'}, {'line': 54, 'text': '                best = path'}, {'line': 55, 'text': '            for nxt in adj[cur]:'}, {'line': 56, 'text': '                if nxt not in path:'}, {'line': 57, 'text': '                    stack.append((nxt, path+[nxt]))'}, {'line': 58, 'text': '    if len(best) < 4:'}, {'line': 59, 'text': '        raise RuntimeError("Could not find a four-carbon chain; carbons=%s adj=%s" % (carbons, adj))'}, {'line': 60, 'text': '    return best[:4]'}, {'line': 61, 'text': ''}, {'line': 62, 'text': ''}, {'line': 63, 'text': 'def write_input(angle, idxs):'}, {'line': 64, 'text': '    i,j,k,l = idxs'}, {'line': 65, 'text': '    inp_name = "scan_%s.inp" % LABELS[angle]'}, {'line': 66, 'text': '    lines = ['}, {'line': 67, 'text': '      "! B3LYP def2-TZVP RKS Opt NoAutoStart",'}, {'line': 68, 'text': '      "%maxcore 192",'}, {'line': 69, 'text': '      "%scf",'}, {'line': 70, 'text': '      "  MaxIter 256",'}, {'line': 71, 'text': '      "  AutoTRAH false",'}, {'line': 72, 'text': '      "end",'}, {'line': 73, 'text': '      "%geom",'}, {'line': 74, 'text': '      "  MaxIter 128",'}, {'line': 75, 'text': '      "  Constraints",'}, {'line': 76, 'text': '      "    { D %d %d %d %d %.1f C }" % (i,j,k,l,angle),'}, {'line': 77, 'text': '      "  end",'}, {'line': 78, 'text': '      "end",'}, {'line': 79, 'text': '      "* xyzfile 0 1 initial.xyz",'}, {'line': 80, 'text': '    ]'}, '[bounded report: additional items omitted]'], 'returned_bytes': 10136, 'status': 'observed', 'coverage': {'version': 'evidence-coverage-1', 'requested': {'kind': 'lines', 'start': 1, 'stop': 201}, 'returned': {'kind': 'lines', 'start': 1, 'stop': 194}, 'complete': True, 'truncated': False, 'reason': None, 'next_cursor': None}}}
